HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_317ef6cdb7a2a48345e1","text":"1. **The self makes murder pliant before the hand performs it.** Fixed **ط و ع / B001** (“pliant obedience and willing yielding”) and **B006** (“making an act easy to oneself”) assign the enabling role to the killer’s *nafs*. The sequence places inner facilitation before killing and immediate loss after it; 5:31 then exposes his incapacity before a simple burial task. I infer that desire rehearses away resistance until the act feels executable. The ayah therefore reads as an account of internal moral engineering, not an unexplained leap from anger to homicide.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B001","root_000839/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_47b13e8592e60241c931","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"5:30:7\",\"root_000839\",[\"B001\"]],[\"5:30:9\",\"root_000409\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sabahın açığa çıkarıcı ışığı, öldürmenin bıraktığı kaybı görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5b6619ad42398891da12","text":"2. **Morning exposes the loss.** `ص ب ح B001` is the opening of dawn and `خ س ر B001` deficiency. The result becomes visible at morning-like disclosure: apparent mastery is revealed as subtraction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ad4c9245e4c568669e1e","text":"1. **The self manufactures ease for the forbidden act.** `ط و ع B006` is making an act easy to oneself, `ن ف س B013` hidden intent, and `ق ت ل B002` subduing until force is softened. I infer that inner rehearsal softens resistance before the external killing occurs. “His self made it compliant” reads as a process of moral friction-removal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B006","root_001200/B002","root_001533/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_afa576b779f28181d0d6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"5:30:1\",\"root_000956\",[\"B006\"]],[\"5:30:3\",\"root_001533\",[\"B013\"]],[\"5:30:4\",\"root_001200\",[\"B002\"]],[\"5:30:6\",\"root_001200\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Benlik, yasak öldürme eylemini el işlemeden önce kolay ve yapılabilir göstererek suçu hazırlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e6625f8722ec13a61f1b","text":"5:31 was the sharp correction: the self that made killing easy immediately confronted inability before the simpler task of covering its result.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ecba9d021d2cf4350da7","text":"Later ayah 5:120 made the fixed ayah's local distinctions newly visible as bounded operations within a single encompassing dominion, rather than as an isolated rule or episode.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
